# Consolidation changes: cote 150

Counts are substantive reading places; a place differing from both witnesses is counted once in Places and once in each witness column. Equivalent TeX syntax and certainty-only changes are excluded from these counts. At unresolved C/H disagreements, counts compare the leading proposed reading with each witness; the confidence and alternatives remain explicit, and such a comparison does not assert a settled error. Certainty-only decisions, where listed below, are identified explicitly. H page references follow its source page marker (scan number minus one).
